<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّهَا أَخْبَرَتْهُ أَنَّ زِيَادَ بْنَ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ كَتَبَ إِلَى عَائِشَةَ زَوْجِ النِّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ قَالَ مَنْ أَهْدَى هَدْيًا حَرُمَ عَلَيْهِ مَا يَحْرُمُ عَلَى الْحَاجِّ حَتَّى يُنْحَرَ الْهَدْىُ وَقَدْ بَعَثْتُ بِهَدْىٍ فَاكْتُبِي إِلَىَّ بِأَمْرِكِ أَوْ مُرِي صَاحِبَ الْهَدْىِ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ عَمْرَةُ قَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ لَيْسَ كَمَا قَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ أَنَا فَتَلْتُ قَلاَئِدَ هَدْىِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِيَدَىَّ ثُمَّ قَلَّدَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِيَدِهِ ثُمَّ بَعَثَ بِهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَعَ أَبِي فَلَمْ يَحْرُمْ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم شَىْءٌ أَحَلَّهُ اللَّهُ لَهُ حَتَّى نُحِرَ الْهَدْىُ ‏.‏</p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from &#x27;Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Muhammad, that Amra bint &#x27;Abd ar-Rahman told him that Ziyad ibn Abi Sufyan once wrote to A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, saying, &quot;&#x27;Abdullah ibn Abbas said that whatever was haram for some one doing hajj was also haram for some one who sent a sacrificial animal until the animal was sacrificed. I have sent one, so write and tell me what you say about this, or tell the man in charge of the animal what to do. Amra said that A&#x27;isha said, &quot;It is notas Ibn Abbas has said. I once plaited the garlands for the sacrificial animal of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, with my own two hands. Then after that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, himself put the garlands on the animal and then sent it with my father. And there was nothing that Allah had made halal forthe Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, that was haram for him until such time as the animal had been sacrificed</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/754" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="auto">उमराह बिन्त अब्दुर रहमान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के ज़ियाद बिन अबी सुफयान ने लिखा उम्मुल मोमिनीन आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) को के अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास कहते हैं जो शख्स हदी </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="auto"> रवाना करे तो उस पर हरम हो गयीं वो चीजें जो हरम हैं मुहरिम पर, यहाँ तक के ज़िबह की जाए हदी। सो मैंने एक हदी तुम्हारे पास रवाना की है, तुम मुझे लिख भेजो अपना फतवा या जो शख्स हदी लेकर आता है उसके हाथ कहला भेजो। </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="auto">उमराह ने कहा आयशा बोलीं: इब्न अब्बास जो कहते हैं वैसा नहीं है। मैंने खुद अपने हाथ से रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की हदी के हार बांटे थे और रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अपने हाथ से लटकायी और उसको रवाना किया मेरे बाप के साथ, सो आप पर कोई चीज हरम ना हुई उन चीजों में से जिनको हलाल किया था अल्लाह ने उनके लिए, यहाँ तक के ज़िबह हो गयी हदी।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 754]</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="rtl">عمرہ بنت عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ زیاد بن ابی سفیان نے لکھا ام المومنین عائشہ کو کہ عبداللہ بن عباس کہتے ہیں جو شخص ہدی روانہ کرے تو اس پر حرام ہو گئیں وہ چیزیں جو حرام ہیں محرم پر یہاں تک کہ ذبح کی جائے ہدی سو میں نے ایک ہدی تمہارے پاس روانہ کی ہے تم مجھے لکھ بھیجو اپنا فتوی یا جو شخص ہدی لے کر آتا ہے اس کے ہاتھ کہلا بھیجو، </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="rtl">عمرہ نے کہا عائشہ بولیں ابن عباس جو کہتے ہیں ویسا نہیں ہے میں نے خود اپنے ہاتھ سے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی ہدی کے ہار بٹے تھے اور رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اپنے ہاتھ سے لٹکائی اور اس کو روانہ کیا میرے باپ کے ساتھ سو آپ پر کوئی چیز حرام نہ ہوئی ان چیزوں میں سے جن کو حلال کیا تھا اللہ نے ان کے لئے یہاں تک کہ ذبح ہوگئی ہدی۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 754]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Umrah bint Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Ziyad bin Abi Sufyan ne likha Ummul Momineen Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) ko ke Abdullah bin Abbas kehte hain jo shakhs hadi (qurbani ka janwar) rawana kare toh us par haram ho gayin wo cheezein jo haram hain muhram par, yahan tak ke zibah ki jaye hadi. So maine ek hadi tumhare paas rawana ki hai, tum mujhe likh bhejo apna fatwa ya jo shakhs hadi lekar aata hai uske haath kehla bhejo. Umrah ne kaha Aisha boli: Ibn Abbas jo kehte hain waisa nahi hai. Maine khud apne haath se Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki hadi ke haar bate the aur Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apne haath se latkayi aur usko rawana kiya mere baap ke saath, so aap par koi cheez haram na hui un cheezon mein se jinko halaal kiya tha Allah ne unke liye, yahan tak ke zibah ho gayi hadi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/754" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سَأَلْتُ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَنِ الَّذِي، يَبْعَثُ بِهَدْيِهِ وَيُقِيمُ هَلْ يَحْرُمُ عَلَيْهِ شَىْءٌ فَأَخْبَرَتْنِي أَنَّهَا سَمِعَتْ عَائِشَةَ تَقُولُ لاَ يَحْرُمُ إِلاَّ مَنْ أَهَلَّ وَلَبَّى ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said said, &quot;I asked Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman if there was anything that was haram for someone who sent a sacrificial animal (to Makka) but did not go there himself, and she told me that she had heard A&#x27;isha say, &#x27;It is only some one who goes into ihram for hajj and begins saying the talbiya for whom things are haram</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 755]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/755" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया बिन सईद से रिवायत है के उन्होंने पूछा उमराह बिन्त अब्दुर रहमान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से के जो शख्स हदी रवाना करे मगर खुद ना जाए, क्या उस पर कुछ लाज़िम होता है? वो बोलीं मैंने सुना आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से कहती थीं: मुहरिम नहीं होता मगर जो शख्स एहराम बांधे और लब्बैक कहे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/755" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 753

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A756)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ التَّيْمِيِّ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الْهُدَيْرِ، أَنَّهُ رَأَى رَجُلاً مُتَجَرِّدًا بِالْعِرَاقِ فَسَأَلَ النَّاسَ عَنْهُ فَقَالُوا إِنَّهُ أَمَرَ بِهَدْيِهِ أَنْ يُقَلَّدَ فَلِذَلِكَ تَجَرَّدَ ‏.‏ قَالَ رَبِيعَةُ فَلَقِيتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ فَذَكَرْتُ لَهُ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ بِدْعَةٌ وَرَبِّ الْكَعْبَةِ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَمَّنْ خَرَجَ بِهَدْىٍ لِنَفْسِهِ فَأَشْعَرَهُ وَقَلَّدَهُ بِذِي الْحُلَيْفَةِ وَلَمْ يُحْرِمْ هُوَ حَتَّى جَاءَ الْجُحْفَةَ قَالَ لاَ أُحِبُّ ذَلِكَ وَلَمْ يُصِبْ مَنْ فَعَلَهُ وَلاَ يَنْبَغِي لَهُ أَنْ يُقَلِّدَ الْهَدْىَ وَلاَ يُشْعِرَهُ إِلاَّ عِنْدَ الإِهْلاَلِ إِلاَّ رَجُلٌ لاَ يُرِيدُ الْحَجَّ فَيَبْعَثُ بِهِ وَيُقِيمُ فِي أَهْلِهِ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ هَلْ يَخْرُجُ بِالْهَدْىِ غَيْرُ مُحْرِمٍ فَقَالَ نَعَمْ لاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ أَيْضًا عَمَّا اخْتَلَفَ فِيهِ النَّاسُ مِنَ الإِحْرَامِ لِتَقْلِيدِ الْهَدْىِ مِمَّنْ لاَ يُرِيدُ الْحَجَّ وَلاَ الْعُمْرَةَ فَقَالَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا الَّذِي نَأْخُذُ بِهِ فِي ذَلِكَ قَوْلُ عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ إِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بَعَثَ بِهَدْيِهِ ثُمَّ أَقَامَ فَلَمْ يَحْرُمْ عَلَيْهِ شَىْءٌ مِمَّا أَحَلَّهُ اللَّهُ لَهُ حَتَّى نُحِرَ هَدْيُهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 756]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/756" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Yahya ibn Said, from Muhammad ibn Ibrahim ibn al-Harith at-Taymi, that Rabia ibn Abdullah ibn al-Hudayr once saw a man in a state of ihram in Iraq. So he asked people about him and they said, &quot;He has given directions for his sacrificial animal to be garlanded, and it is for that reason that he has put on ihram .&quot; Rabia said, &quot;I then met Abdullah ibn az- Zubayr and so I mentioned this to him and he said, &#x27;By the Lord of the Kaba, an innovation.&#x27; &quot; Malik was asked about some one who set out with his own sacrificial animal and marked it and garlanded it at Dhu&#x27;l-Hulayfa, but did not go into ihram until he had reached al- Juhfa,and hesaid, &quot;I do not like that, and whoever does so has not acted properly. He should only garland his sacrificial animal, or mark it, when he goes into ihram, unless it is someone who does not intend to do hajj, in which case he sends it off and stays with his family.&quot; Malik was asked if somone who was not in ihram could set out with a sacrificial animal, and he said, &quot;Yes. There is no harm in that.&quot; He was also asked to comment on the different views people had about what became haram for some one who garlanded a sacrificial animal but did not intend to do either hajj or umra, and he said, &quot;What we go by as far as this is concerned is what A&#x27;isha, umm al-muminin said, &#x27;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, sent his sacrificial animal off and did not go there himself, and there was nothing that Allah had made halal for him that was haram for him until the animal had been sacrificed</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 756]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/756" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">राबिया बिन अब्दुल्लाह ने देखा एक शख्स को इराक में कपड़े उतारे हुए, तो पूछा लोगों से इसका सबब। लोगों ने कहा इसने हुक्म किया है अपनी हदी की तकलीद (गले में हार डालना) का, सो इसीलिए सिले हुए कपड़े उतार डाले। राबिया ने कहा मैंने मुलाक़ात की अब्दुल्लाह बिन ज़ुबैर से और ये किस्सा बयान किया, उन्होंने कहा कसम है काबा के रब्ब की, ये अम्र बिदअत है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 756]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/756" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ربیعہ بن عبداللہ نے دیکھا ایک شخص کو عراق میں کپڑے اتارے ہوئے تو پوچھا لوگوں سے اس کا سبب لوگوں نے کہا اس نے حکم کیا ہے اپنی ہدی کی تقلید کا سو اس لئے سلے ہوئے کپڑے اتار ڈالے ربیعہ نے کہا میں نے ملاقات کی عبداللہ بن زبیر سے اور یہ قصہ بیان کیا انہوں نے کہا قسم کعبہ کے رب کی یہ امر بدعت ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 756]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Rabia bin Abdullah ne dekha ek shakhs ko Iraq mein kapde utare hue, toh poocha logon se iska sabab. Logon ne kaha isne hukum kiya hai apni hadi ki taqleed (gale mein haar dalna) ka, so isliye sile hue kapde utaar dale. Rabia ne kaha maine mulaqat ki Abdullah bin Zubair se aur ye qissa bayan kiya, unhone kaha qasam hai Kaaba ke Rabb ki, ye amr bid&#x27;at hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 756]</p>

## Narration 754

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A757)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَقُولُ الْمَرْأَةُ الْحَائِضُ الَّتِي تُهِلُّ بِالْحَجِّ أَوِ الْعُمْرَةِ إِنَّهَا تُهِلُّ بِحَجِّهَا أَوْ عُمْرَتِهَا إِذَا أَرَادَتْ وَلَكِنْ لاَ تَطُوفُ بِالْبَيْتِ وَلاَ بَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ وَهِيَ تَشْهَدُ الْمَنَاسِكَ كُلَّهَا مَعَ النَّاسِ غَيْرَ أَنَّهَا لاَ تَطُوفُ بِالْبَيْتِ وَلاَ بَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ وَلاَ تَقْرَبُ الْمَسْجِدَ حَتَّى تَطْهُرَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/757" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Nafi, that Abdullah ibn Umar used to say, &quot;A menstruating woman who wants to go into ihram to do either hajj or umra can do so if she so wishes, but she cannot do tawaf of the House, nor the say between Safa and Marwa. She can participate in all the rituals along with everybody else, except that she cannot do tawaf of the House, nor the say between Safa and Marwa, nor can she come near the mosque until she is pure</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 757]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/757" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">नाफे से रिवायत है के अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते थे: जो औरत एहराम बांधे हो हज या उमराह का, फिर उसको हैज़ (मेन्सेस) आना शुरू हो जाए, तो वो लब्बैक कहा करे जब उसका जी चाहे और तवाफ न करे और सई न करे सफा मरवा के दरमियाँ। बाकी सब अरकान अदा करे लोगों के साथ, फकत तवाफ और सई न करे और मस्जिद में न जाए जब तक के पाक न हो।</p>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi se riwayat hai ke Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) kehte the: Jo aurat ehram baandhe ho Hajj ya Umrah ka, phir usko haiz (menses) aana shuru ho jaye, toh wo Labbaik kaha kare jab uska jee chahe aur tawaf na kare aur sa&#x27;i na kare Safa Marwa ke darmiyan. Baqi sab arkaan ada kare logon ke saath, fakat tawaf aur sa&#x27;i na kare aur masjid mein na jaye jab tak ke paak na ho.</p>

## Narration 755

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A758)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم اعْتَمَرَ ثَلاَثًا عَامَ الْحُدَيْبِيَةِ وَعَامَ الْقَضِيَّةِ وَعَامَ الْجِعِرَّانَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 758]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/758" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, did umra three times:in the year of Hudaybiya, in the year of al-Qadiyya, and in the year of al-Jiirrana</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 758]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/758" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 758]</p>

## Narration 756

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A759)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لَمْ يَعْتَمِرْ إِلاَّ ثَلاَثًا إِحْدَاهُنَّ فِي شَوَّالٍ وَاثْنَتَيْنِ فِي ذِي الْقَعْدَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 759]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/759" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa, from his father, that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, only did three umras, one of them in Shawwal, and two in Dhu&#x27;l-Qada</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 759]</p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Abd ar-Rahman ibn Harmala al-Aslami, that somebody asked Said ibn al-Musayyab, &quot;Can I do umra before I do hajj?&quot;, and Said said, &quot;Yes, the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, did umra before doing hajj</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Saeed ibn al-Musayyab answering an unnamed questioner by reporting the Prophet’s umra before Hajj, through Abdur Rahman ibn Harmala.. Read the complete Hindi field. The named transmitter reports an actual question and affirmative answer, followed by an explicit finite statement of the Prophet’s umra before Hajj. Keep the entire dialogue and its actual indirect attribution. Do not turn Saeed into a direct eyewitness or invent an omitted connecting chain.</p>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुर रहमान बिन हरमाला असलामी से रिवायत है के एक शख़्स ने पूछा सईद बिन मुसय्यब से के मैं उमराह करूँ क़ब्ल हज के? उन्होंने कहा: हाँ, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उमराह किया क़ब्ल हज के।</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn alKhattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Ibn Shihab, from Said ibn al-Musayyab, that Umar ibn Abi Salama once asked Umar ibn alKhattab for permission to do umra in Shawwal. He gave him permission, so he did umra and then went back to his family, and he did not do hajj</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 761]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिवायत है के उमर बिन अबी सलमा ने इजाज़त माँगी हज़रत उमर से उमराह करने की शव्वाल में, तो इजाज़त दी आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने, तो वोह उमराह कर के लौट आए अपने घर को और हज न किया।</p>

## Narration 759

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A762)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 762]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/762" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 762</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   762 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقْطَعُ التَّلْبِيَةَ فِي الْعُمْرَةِ إِذَا دَخَلَ الْحَرَمَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِيمَنْ أَحْرَمَ مِنَ التَّنْعِيمِ إِنَّهُ يَقْطَعُ التَّلْبِيَةَ حِينَ يَرَى الْبَيْتَ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ يَعْتَمِرُ مِنْ بَعْضِ الْمَوَاقِيتِ وَهُوَ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْمَدِينَةِ أَوْ غَيْرِهِمْ مَتَى يَقْطَعُ التَّلْبِيَةَ قَالَ أَمَّا الْمُهِلُّ مِنَ الْمَوَاقِيتِ فَإِنَّهُ يَقْطَعُ التَّلْبِيَةَ إِذَا انْتَهَى إِلَى الْحَرَمِ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَبَلَغَنِي أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ كَانَ يَصْنَعُ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/762" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: historical report. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The father’s practice and a later explicit abbreviated transmission from Ibn Umar remain separate; no wording is imported into the latter’s “that”. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The father’s practice and a later explicit abbreviated transmission from Ibn Umar remain separate; no wording is imported into the latter’s “that”.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Hisham ibn &#x27;Urwa, that his father would stop saying the talbiya when he entered the Haram, if he was doing &#x27;umra.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik added, &quot;I have heard that Abdullah ibn Umar used to do that</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 762]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/762" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 762</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   762 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said that someone who went into ihram at at-Tanim should stop saying the talbiya when he saw the House. Yahya said that Malik was asked where a man from the people of Madina, or elsewhere, who had begun doing umra at one of the mawaqit, should stop saying the talbiya, and he said, &quot;Someone who goes into ihram at one of the mawaqit should stop saying the talbiya when he arrives at the Haram.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/762" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 763]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/763" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 763</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   763 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ نَوْفَلِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْمُطَّلِبِ، أَنَّهُ حَدَّثَهُ أَنَّهُ، سَمِعَ سَعْدَ بْنَ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ، وَالضَّحَّاكَ بْنَ قَيْسٍ، عَامَ حَجَّ مُعَاوِيَةُ بْنُ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ - وَهُمَا يَذْكُرَانِ التَّمَتُّعَ بِالْعُمْرَةِ إِلَى الْحَجِّ - فَقَالَ الضَّحَّاكُ بْنُ قَيْسٍ لاَ يَفْعَلُ ذَلِكَ إِلاَّ مَنْ جَهِلَ أَمْرَ اللَّهِ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ سَعْدٌ بِئْسَ مَا قُلْتَ يَا ابْنَ أَخِي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ الضَّحَّاكُ فَإِنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ قَدْ نَهَى عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ سَعْدٌ قَدْ صَنَعَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَصَنَعْنَاهَا مَعَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/763" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. This locally supported Prophetic report is preserved as its own exact source passage; companion/jurist discussion outside the selected passage remains source context. No authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, did it, and we did it with him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 763]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/763" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 763</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   763 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Ibn Shihab, that Muhammad ibn Abdullah ibn al-Harith ibn Nawfal ibn Abd al-Muttalib told him that he had heard Sad ibn Abi Waqqas and ad-Dahhak ibn Qays discussing tamattu in between umra and hajj. Ad-Dahhak ibn Qays said, &quot;Only someone who is ignorant of what Allah, the Exalted and Glorified, says would do that.&quot; Whereupon Sad said, &quot;How wrong is what you have just said, son of my brother!&quot; Ad-Dahhak said, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab forbade that,&quot; and Sad said, &quot;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/763" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुहम्मद बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन हरिथ से रिवायत है के उन्होंने सुना साद बिन आबि वकास और ज़ाहक बिन क़ैस से जिस साल मुआवियाह बिन आबि सुफ़ियान ने हज किया और वह दोनों ज़िक्र कर रहे थे तमत्तु का वही करेगा जो अल्लाह के एहकाम से नावाकिफ हो. साद ने कहा बुरी बात कही तुमने ऐ भतिजे मेरे. ज़ाहहक ने कहा कि उम्र बिन खत्ताब ने मना किया तमत्तु से. साद ने जवाब दिया कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इसको किया और हमने भी आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ किया.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/763" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">محمد بن عبداللہ بن حارث سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے سنا سعد بن ابی وقاس اور ضحاک بن قیس سے جس سال معاویہ بن ابی سفیان نے حج کیا اور وہ دونوں ذکر کر رہے تھے تمتع کا تو ضحاک بن قیس نے کہا کہ تمتع وہی کرے گا جو اللہ کے احکام سے ناواقف ہو سعد نے کہا بری بات کہی تم نے اے بھتیجے میرے۔ ضحاک نے کہا کہ عمر بن خطاب نے منع کیا تمتع سے سعد نے جواب دیا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اس کو کیا اور ہم نے بھی آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ کیا ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Muhammad bin Abdullah bin Harith se riwayat hai ke unhone suna Saad bin Abi Waqas aur Zahhak bin Qais se jis saal Muawiyah bin Abi Sufiyan ne Hajj kiya aur woh dono zikr kar rahe thay Tamattu ka, toh Zahhak bin Qais ne kaha ke Tamattu wahi karega jo Allah ke ehkaam se nawaqif ho. Saad ne kaha buri baat kahi tumne ae bhatije mere. Zahhak ne kaha ke Umar bin Khattab ne mana kiya Tamattu se. Saad ne jawab diya ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne isko kiya aur humne bhi aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke sath kiya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/763" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Sadaqa ibn Yasar, that Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;By Allah, I would rather do umra before hajj and sacrifice an animal than do umra after hajj in the month of Dhu&#x27;l-Hijja</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 765</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   765 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ مَنِ اعْتَمَرَ فِي أَشْهُرِ الْحَجِّ فِي شَوَّالٍ أَوْ ذِي الْقَعْدَةِ أَوْ فِي ذِي الْحِجَّةِ قَبْلَ الْحَجِّ ثُمَّ أَقَامَ بِمَكَّةَ حَتَّى يُدْرِكَهُ الْحَجُّ فَهُوَ مُتَمَتِّعٌ إِنْ حَجَّ وَعَلَيْهِ مَا اسْتَيْسَرَ مِنَ الْهَدْىِ فَإِنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ فَصِيَامُ ثَلاَثَةِ أَيَّامٍ فِي الْحَجِّ وَسَبْعَةٍ إِذَا رَجَعَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَذَلِكَ إِذَا أَقَامَ حَتَّى الْحَجِّ ثُمَّ حَجَّ مِنْ عَامِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي رَجُلٍ مِنْ أَهْلِ مَكَّةَ انْقَطَعَ إِلَى غَيْرِهَا وَسَكَنَ سِوَاهَا ثُمَّ قَدِمَ مُعْتَمِرًا فِي أَشْهُرِ الْحَجِّ ثُمَّ أَقَامَ بِمَكَّةَ حَتَّى أَنْشَأَ الْحَجَّ مِنْهَا إِنَّهُ مُتَمَتِّعٌ يَجِبُ عَلَيْهِ الْهَدْىُ أَوِ الصِّيَامُ إِنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ هَدْيًا وَأَنَّهُ لاَ يَكُونُ مِثْلَ أَهْلِ مَكَّةَ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ رَجُلٍ مِنْ غَيْرِ أَهْلِ مَكَّةَ دَخَلَ مَكَّةَ بِعُمْرَةٍ فِي أَشْهُرِ الْحَجِّ وَهُوَ يُرِيدُ الإِقَامَةَ بِمَكَّةَ حَتَّى يُنْشِئَ الْحَجَّ أَمُتَمَتِّعٌ هُوَ فَقَالَ نَعَمْ هُوَ مُتَمَتِّعٌ وَلَيْسَ هُوَ مِثْلَ أَهْلِ مَكَّةَ وَإِنْ أَرَادَ الإِقَامَةَ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّهُ دَخَلَ مَكَّةَ وَلَيْسَ هُوَ مِنْ أَهْلِهَا وَإِنَّمَا الْهَدْىُ أَوِ الصِّيَامُ عَلَى مَنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ مِنْ أَهْلِ مَكَّةَ وَأَنَّ هَذَا الرَّجُلَ يُرِيدُ الإِقَامَةَ وَلاَ يَدْرِي مَا يَبْدُو لَهُ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ وَلَيْسَ هُوَ مِنْ أَهْلِ مَكَّةَ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Abdullah ibn Dinar, that Abdullah ibn Umar used to say, &quot;Anyone that does umra in the months of hajj, that is, in Shawwal, Dhu&#x27;l-Qada, or in Dhu&#x27;l-Hijja before the hajj, and then stays in Makka until the time for hajj, is doing tamattu if he then does hajj. He must sacrifice whatever animal it is easy for him to obtain, and if he cannot find one then he must fast three days during hajj and seven days when he returns.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 765]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/765" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 765</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   765 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;This is only the case if he stays until the hajj and does hajj in that same year.&quot; Malik said that if someone who was from Makka but had stopped living there and gone to live elsewhere, came back to do umra in the months of the hajj and then stayed in Makka to begin hajj there, he was doing tamattu, and had to offer up a sacrificial animal, or fast if he could not find one. He was not the same as the people of Makka. Malik was asked whether someone who was not from Makka and entered Makka to do umra in the months of hajj with the intention of staying on to begin his hajj there was doing tamattu or not, and he said, &quot;Yes, he is doing tamattu, and he is not the same as the people of Makka, even if he has the intention of staying there. This is because he has entered Makka, and is not one of its people, and making a sacrifice or fasting is incumbent on anyone who is not from Makka, and, although he intends to stay, he does not know what possibilities might arise later. He is not one of the people of Makka</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/765" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 765]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/765" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 765</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   765 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से मालिक से, अब्दुल्लाह इब्न दिनार से रिवायत की के अब्दुल्लाह इब्न उमर (र.अ) कहा करते थे: जो कोई हज के महीनों में यानी शव्वाल, ज़ुल-क़ादा या ज़ुल-हिज्जा में हज से पहले उमरा करे, फिर मक्का में हज के वक़्त तक ठहरे, तो वोह तमत्तु कर रहा है अगर वोह बाद में हज कर ले। उस पर क़ुरबानी लाज़िम है जो उसे मयस्सर हो, और अगर न पाए तो हज के दौरान तीन रोज़े और वापसी पर सात रोज़े रखे। मालिक ने कहा: यह तब है अगर वोह हज तक मक्का में ठहरे और इसी साल हज करे। मालिक ने मज़ीद कहा के अगर कोई मक्का का रहने वाला हो लेकिन वहाँ से हिजरत कर के कहीं और चला गया हो, और फिर हज के महीनों में उमरा करने आए और हज तक ठहरे तो वोह तमत्तु करने वाला है और उस पर क़ुरबानी या रोज़े लाज़िम हैं। वोह मक्का वालों जैसा नहीं है। मालिक से पूछा गया के जो मक्का का नहीं और हज के महीनों में उमरा करने आया और हज करने की नियत से ठहर गया तो क्या वोह तमत्तु है? उन्होंने फ़रमाया: हाँ, वोह तमत्तु है और वोह मक्का वालों जैसा नहीं है, चाहे उसकी नियत वहाँ ठहरने की ही क्यों न हो। क्योंकि वोह मक्का में दाख़िल हुआ है और वहाँ का बाशिंदा नहीं है, लिहाज़ा क़ुरबानी या रोज़े उस पर लाज़िम हैं क्योंकि वोह नहीं जानता के क्या हालात पेश आ जाएं।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/765" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   765 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے مالک سے، عبداللہ بن دینار سے روایت کی کہ عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہ کہا کرتے تھے: جو کوئی حج کے مہینوں میں یعنی شوال، ذوالقعدہ یا ذوالحجہ میں حج سے پہلے عمرہ کرے، پھر مکہ میں حج کے وقت تک ٹھہرے، تو وہ تمتع کر رہا ہے اگر وہ بعد میں حج کر لے۔ اس پر قربانی لازم ہے جو اسے میسر ہو، اور اگر نہ پائے تو حج کے دوران تین روزے اور واپسی پر سات روزے رکھے۔ مالک نے کہا: یہ تب ہے اگر وہ حج تک مکہ میں ٹھہرے اور اسی سال حج کرے۔ مالک نے مزید کہا کہ اگر کوئی مکہ کا رہنے والا ہو لیکن وہاں سے ہجرت کر کے کہیں اور چلا گیا ہو، اور پھر حج کے مہینوں میں عمرہ کرنے آئے اور حج تک ٹھہرے تو وہ تمتع کرنے والا ہے اور اس پر قربانی یا روزے لازم ہیں۔ وہ مکہ والوں جیسا نہیں ہے۔ مالک سے پوچھا گیا کہ جو مکہ کا نہیں اور حج کے مہینوں میں عمرہ کرنے آیا اور حج کرنے کی نیت سے ٹھہر گیا تو کیا وہ تمتع ہے؟ انہوں نے فرمایا: ہاں، وہ تمتع ہے اور وہ مکہ والوں جیسا نہیں ہے، چاہے اس کی نیت وہاں ٹھہرنے کی ہی کیوں نہ ہو۔ کیونکہ وہ مکہ میں داخل ہوا ہے اور وہاں کا باشندہ نہیں ہے، لہذا قربانی یا روزے اس پر لازم ہیں کیونکہ وہ نہیں جانتا کہ کیا حالات پیش آ جائیں۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 765</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   765 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Malik se, Abdullah ibn Dinar se riwayat ki ke Abdullah ibn Umar (R.A) kaha karte the: Jo koi hajj ke mahino mein yani Shawwal, Dhu&#x27;l-Qada ya Dhu&#x27;l-Hijja mein hajj se pehle umra kare, phir Makkah mein hajj ke waqt tak thehre, toh woh tamattu kar raha hai agar woh baad mein hajj kar le. Us par qurbani lazim hai jo use mayassar ho, aur agar na paye toh hajj ke dauran teen rozay aur wapsi par saat rozay rakhe. Malik ne kaha: Yeh tab hai agar woh hajj tak Makkah mein thehre aur isi saal hajj kare. Malik ne mazeed kaha ke agar koi Makkah ka rehne wala ho lekin wahan se hijrat kar ke kahin aur chala gaya ho, aur phir hajj ke mahino mein umra karne aaye aur hajj tak thehre toh woh tamattu karne wala hai aur us par qurbani ya rozay lazim hain. Woh Makkah walon jaisa nahi hai. Malik se poocha gaya ke jo Makkah ka nahi aur hajj ke mahino mein umra karne aaya aur hajj karne ki niyat se theher gaya toh kya woh tamattu hai? Unhone farmaya: Haan, woh tamattu hai aur woh Makkah walon jaisa nahi hai, chahe uski niyat wahan theherne ki hi kyun na ho. Kyunki woh Makkah mein dakhil hua hai aur wahan ka bashinda nahi hai, lehaza qurbani ya rozay us par lazim hain kyunki woh nahi janta ke kya halaat pesh aa jayen.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/765" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، يَقُولُ مَنِ اعْتَمَرَ فِي شَوَّالٍ أَوْ ذِي الْقَعْدَةِ أَوْ فِي ذِي الْحِجَّةِ ثُمَّ أَقَامَ بِمَكَّةَ حَتَّى يُدْرِكَهُ الْحَجُّ فَهُوَ مُتَمَتِّعٌ إِنْ حَجَّ وَمَا اسْتَيْسَرَ مِنَ الْهَدْىِ فَمَنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ فَصِيَامُ ثَلاَثَةِ أَيَّامٍ فِي الْحَجِّ وَسَبْعَةٍ إِذَا رَجَعَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/766" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said used to hear Said ibn al-Musayyab say, &quot;Anyone that does umra in Shawwal, Dhu&#x27;l-Qada or Dhu l-Hijja, and then stays in Makka until it is time for the hajj, is doing tamattu if he then does hajj. He must sacrifice whatever animal it is easy for him to obtain, and if he cannot find one then he must fast three days during hajj and seven days when he returns</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 766]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/766" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से मालिक से रिवायत की के यहया इब्न सईद, सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब को कहते हुए सुनते थे: जो कोई शव्वाल, धुल-कदा या धुल-हिज्जा में उमरा करे, फिर हज के वक्त तक मक्का में ठहरे, तो वो तमतत्तु कर रहा है अगर वो बाद में हज कर ले। उस पर क़ुर्बानी लाज़िम है जो उसे मयस्सर हो, और अगर न पाए तो हज के दौरान तीन रोज़े और वापसी पर सात रोज़े रखे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/766" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 764

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A767)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ سُمَىٍّ، مَوْلَى أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ السَّمَّانِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الْعُمْرَةُ إِلَى الْعُمْرَةِ كَفَّارَةٌ لِمَا بَيْنَهُمَا وَالْحَجُّ الْمَبْرُورُ لَيْسَ لَهُ جَزَاءٌ إِلاَّ الْجَنَّةُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 767]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/767" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 767</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   767 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;Someone who does umra in Shawwal, Dhu&#x27;l-Qada or Dhu&#x27;l-Hijja and then goes back to his people, and then returns and does hajj in that same year does not have to sacrifice an animal. Sacrificing an animal is only incumbent on some one who does umra in the months of hajj, and then stays in Makka and then does hajj. A person not from Makka who moves to Makka and establishes his home there and does umra in the months of the hajj and then begins his hajj there is not doing tamattu. He does not have to sacrifice an animal nor does he have to fast. He is in the same position as the people of Makka if he is one of those who are living there.&quot; Malik was asked whether a man from Makka who had gone to live in another town or had been on a journey and then returned to Makka with the intention of staying there, regardless of whether he had a family there or not, and entered it to do umra in the months of the hajj, and then began his hajj there, beginning his umra at the miqat of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, or at a place nearer than that, was doing tamattu or not? Malik answered, &quot;He does not have to sacrifice an animal or fast as someone who is doing tamattu has to do. This is because Allah, the Blessed and Exalted, says in His Book, &#x27;That is for someone whose family are not present at Masjid al-Haram. &#x27; Yahya related to me from Malik, from Sumayy, the mawla of Abu Bakr ibn Abd ar-Rahman, from Abu Salih as-Samman, from Abu Hurayra, that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace ,said, &quot;Umra is an expiation for what is between it and the next umra, and the only reward for an accepted hajj is the Garden</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 767]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/767" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया एक उमरः से लेकर दूसरे उमरः तक कफ़्फ़ारा है उन गुनाहों का जो उन दोनों के बीच में हों और हज-इ-मबरूर का कोई बदला नहीं है सिवाए जन्नत के.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 767]</p>

## Narration 765

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A768)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ سُمَىٍّ، مَوْلَى أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ أَبَا بَكْرِ بْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، يَقُولُ جَاءَتِ امْرَأَةٌ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَتْ إِنِّي قَدْ كُنْتُ تَجَهَّزْتُ لِلْحَجِّ فَاعْتَرَضَ لِي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ اعْتَمِرِي فِي رَمَضَانَ فَإِنَّ عُمْرَةً فِيهِ كَحِجَّةٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 768]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/768" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Sumayy, the mawla of Abu Bakr ibn Abd ar-Rahman, heard Abu Bakribn Abd ar-Rahman say, &quot;A woman came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said, &#x27;I had arranged to do hajj, but I was prevented,&#x27; and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Do umra in Ramadan, for doing umra in it is like doing hajj</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 768]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/768" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू बक्र बिन अब्दुर रहमान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कहते थे एक औरत आयी रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास और कहा मैंने तैयारी की थी हज की फिर कोई आरिज़ा मुझको हो गया तोह हज अदा न कर सकी. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया रमजान में उमरः कर ले क्युंके एक उमरः रमजान में एक हज के बराबर है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 768]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/768" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوبکر بن عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہتے تھے ایک عورت آئی رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس اور کہا میں نے تیاری کی تھی حج کی پھر کوئی عارضہ مجھ کو ہو گیا تو حج ادا نہ کر سکی آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا رمضان میں عمرہ کر لے کیونکہ ایک عمرہ رمضان میں ایک حج کے برابر ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 768]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Bakr bin Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai kehte thay ek aurat aayi Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aur kaha maine tayyari ki thi Hajj ki phir koi aariza mujhko ho gaya toh Hajj ada na kar saki. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya Ramzan mein Umrah kar le kyunke ek Umrah Ramzan mein ek Hajj ke barabar hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 768]</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ افْصِلُوا بَيْنَ حَجِّكُمْ وَعُمْرَتِكُمْ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ أَتَمُّ لِحَجِّ أَحَدِكُمْ وَأَتَمُّ لِعُمْرَتِهِ أَنْ يَعْتَمِرَ فِي غَيْرِ أَشْهُرِ الْحَجِّ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/769" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Nafi, from Abdullah ibn Umar, that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;Keep your hajj separate from your umra. That way your hajj will be more complete. And your umra will be more complete if you do it outside of the months of the hajj</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 769]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/769" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के हज़रात उम्र फरमाते थे जुदाई करो हज और उमरः में ताके हज भी पूरा ऐडा हो और उमरः भी पूरा ऐडा हो और वह इस तरह के हज के महीनो में न करे बालकाय और दिनों में करे. इमाम मालिक को पहुंचा है के हज़रात उस्मान बिन अफ्फान जब उमरः करते तोह कभी अपने ऊंट से न उतरते यहाँ तक के लौट आते मदीना को.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/769" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر فرماتے تھے جدائی کرو حج اور عمرہ میں تاکہ حج بھی پورا ادا ہو اور عمرہ بھی پورا ادا ہو اور وہ اس طرح کہ حج کے مہینوں میں نہ کرے بلکہ اور دنوں میں کرے ۔ امام مالک کو پہنچا ہے کہ حضرت عثمان بن عفان جب عمرہ کرتے تو کبھی اپنے اونٹ سے نہ اترتے یہاں تک کہ لوٹ آتے مدینہ کو۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar farmate thay judai karo Hajj aur Umrah mein taake Hajj bhi pura ada ho aur Umrah bhi pura ada ho aur woh is tarah ke Hajj ke mahino mein na kare balkay aur dino mein kare. Imam Malik ko pahuncha hai ke Hazrat Usman bin Affan jab Umrah karte toh kabhi apne unt se na utarte yahan tak ke laut aate Madina ko.</p>

## Narration 767

<p dir="rtl">الْمِيقَاتِ الَّذِي وَقَّتَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 770]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 770</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   770 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ، كَانَ إِذَا اعْتَمَرَ رُبَّمَا لَمْ يَحْطُطْ عَنْ رَاحِلَتِهِ حَتَّى يَرْجِعَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الْعُمْرَةُ سُنَّةٌ وَلاَ نَعْلَمُ أَحَدًا مِنَ الْمُسْلِمِينَ أَرْخَصَ فِي تَرْكِهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلاَ أَرَى لأَحَدٍ أَنْ يَعْتَمِرَ فِي السَّنَةِ مِرَارًا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْمُعْتَمِرِ يَقَعُ بِأَهْلِهِ إِنَّ عَلَيْهِ فِي ذَلِكَ الْهَدْىَ وَعُمْرَةً أُخْرَى يَبْتَدِئُ بِهَا بَعْدَ إِتْمَامِهِ الَّتِي أَفْسَدَ وَيُحْرِمُ مِنْ حَيْثُ أَحْرَمَ بِعُمْرَتِهِ الَّتِي أَفْسَدَ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ أَحْرَمَ مِنْ مَكَانٍ أَبْعَدَ مِنْ مِيقَاتِهِ فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ أَنْ يُحْرِمَ إِلاَّ مِنْ مِيقَاتِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمَنْ دَخَلَ مَكَّةَ بِعُمْرَةٍ فَطَافَ بِالْبَيْتِ وَسَعَى بَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ وَهُوَ جُنُبٌ أَوْ عَلَى غَيْرِ وُضُوءٍ ثُمَّ وَقَعَ بِأَهْلِهِ ثُمَّ ذَكَرَ - قَالَ - يَغْتَسِلُ أَوْ يَتَوَضَّأُ ثُمَّ يَعُودُ فَيَطُوفُ بِالْبَيْتِ وَيَسْعَى بَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ وَيَعْتَمِرُ عُمْرَةً أُخْرَى وَيُهْدِي وَعَلَى الْمَرْأَةِ إِذَا أَصَابَهَا زَوْجُهَا وَهِيَ مُحْرِمَةٌ مِثْلُ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَأَمَّا الْعُمْرَةُ مِنَ التَّنْعِيمِ فَإِنَّهُ مَنْ شَاءَ أَنْ يَخْرُجَ مِنَ الْحَرَمِ ثُمَّ يُحْرِمَ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ مُجْزِئٌ عَنْهُ إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ وَلَكِنِ الْفَضْلُ أَنْ يُهِلَّ مِنَ </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl"> أَوْ مَا هُوَ أَبْعَدُ مِنَ التَّنْعِيمِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="ltr">the miqat which the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 770]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 770</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   770 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Uthman ibn Affan would sometimes never get down from the animal he was riding on when he was doing umra, until he had returned . Malik said, &#x27;&#x27;Umra is a sunna, and we do not know of any muslim who has ever said that it is permissible not to do it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I do not think that anyone can do more than one umra in any one year.&quot; Malik said that someone doing umra who had sexual intercourse with his wife had to sacrifice an animal and do a second umra, which he had to begin when he had finished the one that he had spoiled. He should go into ihram at the same place where he went into ihram for the umra which he had spoiled, except if he had entered into ihram at a place further away than his miqat. This was because he only had to go into ihram from his miqat. Malik said, &quot;Someone who entered Makka to do umra, and does tawaf of the House and say between Safa and Marwa while he is junub, or not in wudu, and afterwards has intercourse with his wife, and then remembers, should do ghusl, or wudu, and then go back and do tawaf around the House and say between Safa and Marwa and do another umra and sacrifice an animal. A woman should do the same if her husband has intercourse with her while she is in ihram. &quot; Malik said, &quot;As for beginning umra at at-Tanim, (it is not the only alternative). It is permissible if Allah wills for some one to leave the Haram and go into ihram if he wishes, but the best way is for him to go into ihram at </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> (i.e. at-Tanim), or one which is further away</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 770]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 770</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   770 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझसे इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की के उन्होंने सुना के उस्मान इब्न अफ्फ़ान (रज़ि) कभी कभी उमराह करते वक़्त अपनी सवारी से नहीं उतरते थे यहाँ तक के वापस आ जाते। इमाम मालिक ने कहा, उमराह एक सुन्नत है और हम किसी ऐसे मुसलमान को नहीं जानते जिसने ये कहा हो के इसे न करना जाइज़ है। इमाम मालिक ने कहा, मेरा नहीं ख़याल के कोई शख़्स एक साल में एक से ज़्यादा उमराह कर सकता है। इमाम मालिक ने कहा के जो शख़्स उमराह कर रहा हो और अपनी बीवी से हमबिस्तरी कर ले, उसपर एक जानवर की क़ुर्बानी वाजिब है और दूसरा उमराह करना होगा, जिसे उसे पहले वाले ख़राब शुदा उमरे को मुकम्मल करने के बाद शुरू करना चाहिए। इसे एहराम उसी जगह से बांधना चाहिए जहां से उसने ख़राब शुदा उमरे के लिए बांधा था, सिवाए इसके के उसने मीक़ात से बहुत दूर से एहराम बांधा हो। इसकी वजह ये है के उसे सिर्फ़ मीक़ात से ही एहराम बांधना था। इमाम मालिक ने कहा, जो शख़्स मक्का में उमराह करने के लिए दाख़िल हो और तवाफ़-ए-काबा और सफ़ा ओ मरवा के दरम्यान सई इस हालत में करे के वो जुनुबी हो या बा-वुज़ू न हो, और बाद में अपनी बीवी से हमबिस्तरी कर ले और फिर उसे याद आए, तो उसे गुस्ल या वुज़ू करना चाहिए और फिर वापस जा कर तवाफ़-ए-काबा और सफ़ा ओ मरवा के दरम्यान सई करनी चाहिए और दूसरा उमराह कर के जानवर की क़ुर्बानी देनी चाहिए। एक औरत को भी यही करना चाहिए अगर उसका शौहर उसके एहराम की हालत में उस से हमबिस्तरी करे। इमाम मालिक ने कहा, जहां तक तनीम से उमराह शुरू करने की बात है, तो ये वाहिद मुतबदिल नहीं है। अगर अल्लाह चाहे तो ये जाइज़ है के कोई हरम से बाहर निकल कर एहराम बांधे, लेकिन बेहतरीन तरीक़ा ये है के वो उस मीक़ात से एहराम बांधे जिसे अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इस्तेमाल किया (यानी तनीम), या उस से भी दूर से।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 770]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 770</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   770 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی کہ انہوں نے سنا کہ عثمان بن عفان رضی اللہ عنہ کبھی کبھی عمرہ کرتے وقت اپنی سواری سے نہیں اترتے تھے یہاں تک کہ واپس آ جاتے۔ امام مالک نے کہا، عمرہ ایک سنت ہے اور ہم کسی ایسے مسلمان کو نہیں جانتے جس نے یہ کہا ہو کہ اسے نہ کرنا جائز ہے۔ امام مالک نے کہا، میرا نہیں خیال کہ کوئی شخص ایک سال میں ایک سے زیادہ عمرہ کر سکتا ہے۔ امام مالک نے کہا کہ جو شخص عمرہ کر رہا ہو اور اپنی بیوی سے ہمبستری کر لے، اس پر ایک جانور کی قربانی واجب ہے اور دوسرا عمرہ کرنا ہوگا، جسے اسے پہلے والے خراب شدہ عمرے کو مکمل کرنے کے بعد شروع کرنا چاہیے۔ اسے احرام اسی جگہ سے باندھنا چاہیے جہاں سے اس نے خراب شدہ عمرے کے لیے باندھا تھا، سوائے اس کے کہ اس نے میقات سے بہت دور سے احرام باندھا ہو۔ اس کی وجہ یہ ہے کہ اسے صرف میقات سے ہی احرام باندھنا تھا۔ امام مالک نے کہا، جو شخص مکہ میں عمرہ کرنے کے لیے داخل ہو اور طوافِ کعبہ اور صفا و مروہ کے درمیان سعی اس حالت میں کرے کہ وہ جنبی ہو یا باوضو نہ ہو، اور بعد میں اپنی بیوی سے ہمبستری کر لے اور پھر اسے یاد آئے، تو اسے غسل یا وضو کرنا چاہیے اور پھر واپس جا کر طوافِ کعبہ اور صفا و مروہ کے درمیان سعی کرنی چاہیے اور دوسرا عمرہ کر کے جانور کی قربانی دینی چاہیے۔ ایک عورت کو بھی یہی کرنا چاہیے اگر اس کا شوہر اس کے احرام کی حالت میں اس سے ہمبستری کرے۔ امام مالک نے کہا، جہاں تک تنعیم سے عمرہ شروع کرنے کی بات ہے، تو یہ واحد متبادل نہیں ہے۔ اگر اللہ چاہے تو یہ جائز ہے کہ کوئی حرم سے باہر نکل کر احرام باندھے، لیکن بہترین طریقہ یہ ہے کہ وہ اس میقات سے احرام باندھے جسے اللہ کے رسول (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے استعمال کیا (یعنی تنعیم)، یا اس سے بھی دور سے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 770]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 770</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   770 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhse Imam Malik se riwayat ki ke unhone suna ke Uthman ibn Affan (RA) kabhi kabhi umrah karte waqt apni sawari se nahi utarte the yahan tak ke wapas aa jate. Imam Malik ne kaha, Umrah ek sunnat hai aur hum kisi aise musalman ko nahi jante jisne ye kaha ho ke ise na karna jaiz hai. Imam Malik ne kaha, mera nahi khayal ke koi shakhs ek saal mein ek se zyada umrah kar sakta hai. Imam Malik ne kaha ke jo shakhs umrah kar raha ho aur apni biwi se humbistari kar le, uspar ek janwar ki qurbani wajib hai aur dusra umrah karna hoga, jise use pehle wale kharab shuda umre ko mukammal karne ke baad shuru karna chahiye. Ise ihram usi jagah se bandhna chahiye jahan se usne kharab shuda umre ke liye bandha tha, siwaye iske ke usne miqat se bahut door se ihram bandha ho. Iski wajah ye hai ke use sirf miqat se hi ihram bandhna tha. Imam Malik ne kaha, jo shakhs Makkah mein umrah karne ke liye dakhil ho aur tawaf-e-Ka&#x27;ba aur Safa o Marwa ke darmiyan sa&#x27;i is halat mein kare ke wo junubi ho ya ba-wuzu na ho, aur baad mein apni biwi se humbistari kar le aur phir use yaad aaye, to use ghusl ya wuzu karna chahiye aur phir wapas ja kar tawaf-e-Ka&#x27;ba aur Safa o Marwa ke darmiyan sa&#x27;i karni chahiye aur dusra umrah kar ke janwar ki qurbani deni chahiye. Ek aurat ko bhi yahi karna chahiye agar uska shohar uske ihram ki halat mein us se humbistari kare. Imam Malik ne kaha, jahan tak Tanim se umrah shuru karne ki baat hai, to ye wahid mutabadil nahi hai. Agar Allah chahe to ye jaiz hai ke koi Haram se bahar nikal kar ihram bandhe, lekin behtareen tariqa ye hai ke wo us miqat se ihram bandhe jise Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne istemal kiya (yani Tanim), ya us se bhi door se.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/770" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 768

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A771)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بَعَثَ أَبَا رَافِعٍ وَرَجُلاً مِنَ الأَنْصَارِ فَزَوَّجَاهُ مَيْمُونَةَ بِنْتَ الْحَارِثِ وَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِالْمَدِينَةِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَخْرُجَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 771]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/771" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 771</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   771 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman, from Sulayman ibn Yasar, that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, sent Abu Rafi and a man of the Ansar to arrange his marriage to Maymuna bint al-Harith, and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was in Madina before he had left for umra</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 771]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/771" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसार से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अपने मौला अबू राफ़े और एक शख़्स अंसारी को भेजा, उन दोनों ने निकाह कर दिया उनका मैमूनह बिंत हारिस से, और रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मदीना में थे निकलने से पहले।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 771]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/771" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اپنے مولیٰ ابورافع اور ایک شخص انصاری کو بھیجا ان دونوں نے نکاح کر دیا ان کا میمونہ بنت حارث سے اور رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم مدینہ میں تھے نکلنے سے پہلے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 771]</p>

## Narration 769

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A772)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ نُبَيْهِ بْنِ وَهْبٍ، أَخِي بَنِي عَبْدِ الدَّارِ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، أَرْسَلَ إِلَى أَبَانَ بْنِ عُثْمَانَ - وَأَبَانُ يَوْمَئِذٍ أَمِيرُ الْحَاجِّ وَهُمَا مُحْرِمَانِ - إِنِّي قَدْ أَرَدْتُ أَنْ أُنْكِحَ طَلْحَةَ بْنَ عُمَرَ بِنْتَ شَيْبَةَ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ وَأَرَدْتُ أَنْ تَحْضُرَ ‏.‏ فَأَنْكَرَ ذَلِكَ عَلَيْهِ أَبَانُ وَقَالَ سَمِعْتُ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ يَقُولُ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَنْكِحِ الْمُحْرِمُ وَلاَ يُنْكِحْ وَلاَ يَخْطُبْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 772]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/772" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 772</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   772 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Nafi, from Nubayh ibn Wahb, who was from the tribe of Bani Abd ad-Dar, that Umar ibn Ubaydullah sent a message to Aban ibn Uthman (who was amir of the hajj at the time), while both of them were in ihram, saying, &quot;I want to marry Bint Shayba ibn Jubayr to Talha ibn Umar and I want you to be present.&quot; Aban told him that he should not do that and said, &quot;I heard Uthman ibn Affan say that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;A man in ihram should not marry, or give in marriage, or get betrothed</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 772]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/772" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 772</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   772 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">नबीह बिन वहब से रिवायत है के उमर बिन उबैदुल्लाह ने उन्हें अबान बिन उस्मान के पास भेजा और अबान उन दिनों में अमीर थे हाजियों के और दोनों एहराम बांधे हुए थे। कहला भेजा के मैं चाहता हूँ के निकाह करूँ तलहा बिन उमर का शैबा बिन जुबैर की बेटी से, सो तुम भी आओ। अबान ने इस पर इनकार किया और कहा के सुना मैंने उस्मान बिन अफ्फ़ान से, उन्होंने कहा सुना मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से फ़रमाते हुए, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: न निकाह करे मोह्रिम अपना और न ग़ैर का और न पैग़ाम भेजे निकाह का।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 772]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/772" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 772</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   772 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">نبیہ بن وہب سے روایت ہے کہ عمر بن عبیداللہ نے بھیجا ان کو ابان بن عثمان کے پاس اور ابان ان دنوں میں امیر تھے حاجیوں کے اور دونوں احرام باندھے ہوئے تھے کہلا بھیجا کہ میں چاہتا ہوں کہ نکاح کروں طلحہ بن عمر کا شیبہ بن جبیر کی بیٹی سے سو تم بھی آؤ، ابان نے اس پر انکار کیا اور کہا کہ سنا میں نے عثمان بن عفان سے انہوں نے کہا سنا میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے فرماتے تھے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نہ نکاح کرے محرم اپنا اور نہ غیر کا اور نہ پیغام بھیجے نکاح کا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 772]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/772" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 772</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   772 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Nabih bin Wahb se riwayat hai ke Umar bin Ubaidullah ne unhe Aban bin Usman ke paas bheja aur Aban un dino mein ameer the hajiyon ke aur dono ehram bandhe hue the. Kehla bheja ke main chahta hoon ke nikah karun Talha bin Umar ka Shaiba bin Jubair ki beti se, so tum bhi aao. Aban ne is par inkar kiya aur kaha ke suna maine Usman bin Affan se, unhone kaha suna maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se farmate hue, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Na nikah kare mohrim apna aur na ghair ka aur na paigham bheje nikah ka.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 772]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/772" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 770

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ دَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ، أَنَّ أَبَا غَطَفَانَ بْنَ طَرِيفٍ الْمُرِّيَّ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ أَبَاهُ طَرِيفًا تَزَوَّجَ امْرَأَةً وَهُوَ مُحْرِمٌ فَرَدَّ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ نِكَاحَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/773" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Da&#x27;ud ibn al-Husayn, that Abu Ghatafan ibn Tarif al-Murri told him that his father Tarif had married a woman while he was in ihram, and Umar ibn al-Khattab had rescinded the marriage</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 773]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/773" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 771

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَقُولُ لاَ يَنْكِحِ الْمُحْرِمُ وَلاَ</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Nafi, that Abdullah ibn Umar used to say, &quot;Someone in ihram may neither get married, nor arrange a marriage for himself or others</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 774]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/774" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَسَالِمَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، سُئِلُوا عَنْ نِكَاحِ الْمُحْرِمِ، فَقَالُوا لاَ يَنْكِحِ الْمُحْرِمُ وَلاَ يُنْكِحْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ الْمُحْرِمِ إِنَّهُ يُرَاجِعُ امْرَأَتَهُ إِنْ شَاءَ إِذَا كَانَتْ فِي عِدَّةٍ مِنْهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/775" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor); Salim ibn Abdullah (successor); Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab, Salim ibn Abdullah and Sulayman ibn Yasar were asked about whether someone in ihram could get married, and they said, &quot;Some one in ihram may neither get married nor give some one in marriage.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 775]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/775" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 775</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   775 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said that a man who was in ihram could return to his wife if he wanted to, if she was still in her idda after she had been divorced from him</p>

<details>

<p dir="auto">नफ़ी से रिवायत है के अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर कहते थे न निकाह करे मोहरिम और न पैग़ाम भेजे अपना और न घेर का. इमाम मालिक को पहुंचा के सईद बिन मुसय्यब और सलीम बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन सुलैमान बिन यसर से सवाल हुआ मोहरिम के निकाह का तो उन सब ने कहा मोहरिम न निकाह करे अपना न पराया.</p>

## Narration 773

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A776)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم احْتَجَمَ وَهُوَ مُحْرِمٌ فَوْقَ رَأْسِهِ وَهُوَ يَوْمَئِذٍ بِلَحْيَىْ جَمَلٍ مَكَانٌ بِطَرِيقِ مَكَّةَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 776]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/776" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 776</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   776 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Yahya ibn Sa&#x27;id, from Sulayman ibn Yasar, that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was once cupped on the top of his head while he was in ihram, at Lahyay Jamal, which is a place on the road to Makka</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 776]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/776" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसर से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पचने लगाए ऐहराम में अपने सर पर लहि जमल में जो एक मक़ाम है मक्का की राह में.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 776]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/776" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پچھنے لگائے احرام میں اپنے سر پر لحی جمل میں جو ایک مقام ہے مکہ کی راہ میں</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ لاَ يَحْتَجِمُ الْمُحْرِمُ إِلاَّ مِمَّا لاَ بُدَّ لَهُ مِنْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ يَحْتَجِمُ الْمُحْرِمُ إِلاَّ مِنْ ضَرُورَةٍ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Nafi, that Abdullah ibn Umar used to say, &quot;Someone in ihram should not be cupped, except when there is no other alternative.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 777]</p>
