## Narration 775

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A778)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 778]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 778</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   778 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ التَّيْمِيِّ عَنْ نَافِعٍ، مَوْلَى أَبِي قَتَادَةَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ عَنْ أَبِي قَتَادَةَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم حَتَّى إِذَا كَانُوا بِبَعْضِ طَرِيقِ مَكَّةَ تَخَلَّفَ مَعَ أَصْحَابٍ لَهُ مُحْرِمِينَ وَهُوَ غَيْرُ مُحْرِمٍ فَرَأَى حِمَارًا وَحْشِيًّا فَاسْتَوَى عَلَى فَرَسِهِ فَسَأَلَ أَصْحَابَهُ أَنْ يُنَاوِلُوهُ سَوْطَهُ فَأَبَوْا عَلَيْهِ فَسَأَلَهُمْ رُمْحَهُ فَأَبَوْا فَأَخَذَهُ ثُمَّ شَدَّ عَلَى الْحِمَارِ فَقَتَلَهُ فَأَكَلَ مِنْهُ بَعْضُ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَأَبَى بَعْضُهُمْ فَلَمَّا أَدْرَكُوا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم سَأَلُوهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّمَا هِيَ طُعْمَةٌ أَطْعَمَكُمُوهَا اللَّهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. This locally supported Prophetic report is preserved as its own exact source passage; companion/jurist discussion outside the selected passage remains source context. No authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, from Abu&#x27;n-Nadr, the mawla of &#x27;Umar ibn &#x27;Ubaydullah at-Taymi, from Nafi, the mawla of Abu Qatada al- Ansari, that Abu Qatada was once with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. When they got to one of the roads to Makka he fell behind with some companions of his who were muhrim, while he was not. Then he saw a wild ass, so he got on his mount and asked his companions to give him his whip but they refused. Then he asked them for his spear and they refused to give it to him. So he took hold of it and attacked the ass and killed it. Some of the companions of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, ate from it, and others refused. When they had caught up with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, they asked him about it and he said, &quot;It is food that Allah has fed you with</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 778]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 778]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 778</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   778 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू क़तादा अंसारी से रिवायत है के वोह आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ थे मक्का के एक रास्ते में, पीछे रह गया वोह (अबू क़तादा) अपने चंद साथियों के साथ जो एहराम बांधे हुए थे लेकिन वोह (अबू क़तादा) एहराम नहीं बांधे हुए थे। उन्होंने एक गोर-खर (wild ass) को देखा तो अपने घोड़े पर सवार हुए और साथियों से कोड़ा मांगा, उन्होंने इनकार किया, फिर बरछा मांगा उन्होंने इनकार किया। आखिर उन्होंने खुद बरछा लेकर हमला किया गोर-खर पर और क़त्ल किया उसको और बाज़ सहाबा ने वोह गोश्त खाया और बाज़ ने इनकार किया। जब रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से मिले तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से बयान किया, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: वोह एक खाना था जो खिलाया तुमको अल्लाह जल्ला जलालुहू ने।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 778]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 778</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   778 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابو قتادہ انصاری سے روایت ہے کہ وہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ تھے مکہ کے ایک راستے میں ، پیچھے رہ گیا وہ (ابوقتادہ) اپنے چند ساتھیوں کے ساتھ جو احرام باندھے ہوئے تھے لیکن وہ (ابوقتادہ) احرام نہیں باندھے ہوئے تھے انہوں نے ایک گورخر کو دیکھا تو اپنے گھوڑے پر سوار ہوئے اور ساتھیوں سے کوڑا مانگا انہوں نے انکار کیا پھر برچھا مانگا انہوں نے انکار کیا آخر انہوں نے خود برچھا لے کر حملہ کیا گورخر پر اور قتل کیا اس کو اور بعض صحابہ نے وہ گوشت کھایا اور بعض نے انکار کیا جب رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے ملے تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے بیان کیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا وہ ایک کھانا تھا جو کھلایا تم کو اللہ جل جلالہ نے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 778]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 778</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   778 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Qatada Ansari se riwayat hai ke woh aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke saath the Makka ke ek raaste mein, peeche reh gaya woh (Abu Qatada) apne chand sathiyon ke saath jo ehram bandhe hue the lekin woh (Abu Qatada) ehram nahi bandhe hue the. Unhone ek gor-khar (wild ass) ko dekha to apne ghode par sawar hue aur sathiyon se koda manga, unhone inkar kiya, phir barchha manga unhone inkar kiya. Aakhir unhone khud barchha lekar hamla kiya gor-khar par aur qatl kiya usko aur baaz sahaba ne woh gosht khaya aur baaz ne inkar kiya. Jab Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se mile to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se bayan kiya, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Woh ek khana tha jo khilaya tumko Allah Jalla Jalaluhu ne.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/778" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
